such, the amount of purchase orders may vary significantly from time to time,       此，購買訂單的金額不時會有大額差異，難以
and it is difficult to forecast the amount of orders to be received by the Group    預測本集團未來會收到的訂單金額。概不保證

prior periods, if at all. As a result, the results of operations of the Group may   因此，本集團的營運業績在日後或會有重大差

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT
企業管治報告

The Board recognises the importance of corporate governance practice of a         董事會明瞭上市公司企業管治常規之重要性。
listed company. It is in the interest of the stakeholders and shareholders for    上市公司之營運具透明度，採納各種自行規管
a listed company to operate in a transparent manner with the adoptions of         政策與程序以及監控機制，並清楚界定董事與
various self-regulatory policies, procedures and monitoring mechanisms and        管理層權責，乃符合權益持有人及股東之利益。
a clear definition of accountability of directors and management.

The Company had adopted and complied with the code provisions as set out          本公司於截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年
in Part 2 of Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules throughout the year ended 31        度年內已採納並一直遵守上市規則附錄C1第二
December 2025.                                                                    部分所載的守則條文。

In addition to applying the principles in the CG Code, which is mandatory          除屬強制性質之企業管治守則外，董事會亦參
in nature, the Board also observes certain recommended best practices              考上市規則附錄C1所載之若干建議最佳常規
(“Recommended Best Practices”) contained in Appendix C1 to the Listing            （「建議最佳常規」），並採納適合本公司現行情
Rules and has adopted certain Recommended Best Practices which are                 況之若干建議最佳常規。董事會將於合適及適

enhance the corporate governance standard of the Company by reference to           之企業管治水平。
the Recommended Best Practices whenever suitable and appropriate.

Having made specific enquiry of all directors, each director has confirmed that   等於截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度年

                                          •       Members of all board committees are independent non-executive Directors.

     Independent non-executive            •       The directors’ nomination policy of the Company sets a maximum tenure of
       Directors’ tenure                          nine consecutive years for independent non-executive Directors unless the
                                                  Board determines that such Director is still independent.

                                                  董事會認為該董事仍屬獨立人士。

     Group and contribution to the Group’s overall business performance.             授人優化其表現和績效。薪酬委員會認

     business performance of the Group, to which the Grantees would                  表現，以及若本公司股價上升，承授
     directly contribute, and the grantees will benefit more from the Share          人將從購股權獲益更多；(ii)授予各承授
     Options if the Company’s share price increases; (ii) the number of              人的購股權數量是在考慮其過往財務
     share options granted to each grantee was determined after taking               期間的貢獻後確定的，故此在決定授
     into account their contribution for the past financial periods, such            予任何購股權之前已達到相關績效目
     that relevant performance targets have already been met before any              標。購股權亦須遵守該計劃的條款，該

     will lapse automatically if the grantee ceases to be an employee or             酬委員會認為，考慮到該計劃已就中止

      Kingboard Holdings Limited (the “Company”) is a public limited                 建滔集團有限公司（「本公司」）在開曼群
      company incorporated in the Cayman Islands and its shares are listed           島註冊成立為上市有限公司，其股份在

      (the “Stock Exchange”). The addresses of the registered office and             主板上市。本公司註冊辦事處之地址及
      principal place of business of the Company are disclosed in the                主要營業地點於本年報「公司資料」一節

      The Company is an investment holding company and the principal                 本公司為投資控股公司，其附屬公司之
      activities of its subsidiaries are set out in Note 44.                         主要業務載於附註44。

      magnetic products, property development and investments and                    化工產品、磁電產品、房地產發展及投
      investment business.                                                           資以及投資業務。

      The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong Kong               綜合財務報表以港元（「港元」）呈列，港
      dollars (“HK$”), which is different from the Company’s functional              元有別於本公司的功能貨幣人民幣（「人
      currency of Renminbi (“RMB”), the currency of the primary economic             民幣」），即本公司經營所在的主要經濟
      environment in which the Company operates. As the Company’s                    環境的貨幣。由於本公司股份於聯交所

     Amendments to HKFRS 9 and HKFRS 7 “Amendments to the                            香港財務報告準則第9號及香港財務報

                                                                                               「金融工具的分類
                                                                                     及計量（修訂本）」
     The amendments to HKFRS 9 clarify the recognition and                           香港財務報告準則第9號（修訂本）就金
     derecognition for financial asset and financial liability and add an            融資產及金融負債之確認及終止確認進
     exception which permits an entity to deem a financial liability to be           行澄清，並增訂例外情況，倘金融負債
     discharged before the settlement date if it is settled in cash using an         透過電子支付系統以現金結算，且僅在
     electronic payment system if, and only if certain conditions are met.           滿足若干條件之情況下，允許實體視該
                                                                                     金融負債於結算日前已獲解除。

     The amendments also provide guidance on the assessment of                       該等修訂本亦就評估金融資產之合約現
     whether the contractual cash flows of a financial asset are consistent          金流量是否符合基本借貸安排提供指
     with a basic lending arrangement. The amendments specify that                   引。該等修訂本訂明，實體應專注於實
     an entity should focus on what an entity is being compensated for               體獲得之補償物，而非補償金額。倘合
     rather than the compensation amount. Contractual cash flows are                 約現金流量與不屬於基本借貸風險或成
     inconsistent with a basic lending arrangement if they are indexed to a          本之變量掛鈎，則其與基本借貸安排不
     variable that is not a basic lending risk or cost. The amendments state         一致。該等修訂本說明，在部分情況
     that, in some cases, a contingent feature may give rise to contractual          下，某項或然特徵可能會引致合約現金
     cash flows that are consistent with a basic lending arrangement both            流量在合約現金流量變動之前及之後與
     before and after the change in contractual cash flows, but the nature           基本借貸安排一致，但或然事件本身之
     of the contingent event itself does not relate directly to changes in           性質與基本借貸風險及成本之變動並無
     basic lending risks and costs. Furthermore, the description of the term         直接關係。此外，「無追索權」一詞之描
     “non-recourse” is enhanced and the characteristics of “contractually            述在該等修訂本中獲得加強，而「合約
     linked instruments” are clarified in the amendments.                            掛鈎工具」之特點亦在該等修訂本中澄
                                                                                     清。

     The disclosure requirements in HKFRS 7 “Financial Instruments”                  香港財務報告準則第7號「金融工具：披
     Disclosures” (“HKFRS 7”) in respect of investments in equity                    露」
                                                                                      （「香港財務報告準則第7號」）有關指
     instruments designated at fair value through other comprehensive                定為按公平值計入其他全面收益之權益
     income are amended. In particular, entities are required to disclose            工具之投資的披露規定已予修訂。具體
     the fair value gain or loss presented in other comprehensive income             而言，實體須披露於期內在其他全面收
     during the period, showing separately those related to investments              益內呈列的公平值收益或虧損，分別列
     derecognised during the reporting period and those related to                   示有關報告期內已終止確認投資之公平
     investments held at the end of the reporting period. An entity is also          值收益或虧損以及有關於報告期末持有
     required to disclose any transfers of the cumulative gain or loss within        投資之公平值收益或虧損。實體亦須披
     equity related to the investments derecognised during the reporting             露與於報告期內終止確認之投資有關之
     period. In addition, the amendments introduce the requirements of               累計收益或虧損在權益內之任何轉撥。
     qualitative and quantitative disclosure of contractual terms that could         此外，修訂本亦引入有關合約條款的定
     affect the contractual cash flow based on a contingent even not                 性及定量披露規定，該等條款可能基於
     directly relating to basic lending risks and cost.                              與基本借貸風險及成本並無直接關係之
                                                                                     或有事件對合約現金流量構成影響。

     The amendments are effective for annual reporting periods beginning             該等修訂本於二零二六年一月一日或之
     on or after 1 January 2026, with early application permitted. The               後開始之年度報告期間生效，並允許提
     application of the amendments is not expected to have significant               前應用。預期應用該等修訂不會對本集
     impact on the financial position and performance of the Group.                  團的財務狀況及表現造成重大影響。

     1, introduces new requirements to present specified categories and               項新訂香港財務報告準則沿用香港會計

     on management-defined performance measures in the notes to the                   新規定，要求於損益表中呈列指定類別

     Policies, Changes in Accounting Estimates and Errors” and HKFRS 7.               香港會計準則第1號的若干段落已移至

     33 “Earnings per Share” are also made.                                           計變動及差錯」及香港財務報告準則第7

                                                                                      出輕微修訂。

     HKFRS 18, and amendments to other standards, will be effective                   香港財務報告準則第18號及其他準則
     for annual periods beginning on or after 1 January 2027, with                    的修訂本將於二零二七年一月一日或之
     early application permitted. The application of the new standard is              後開始的年度期間生效，並允許提前應
     expected to affect the presentation of the statement of profit or loss           用。預期應用該新準則將會影響日後財
     and disclosures in the future financial statements. The Group is in              務報表中損益表的呈列及披露。本集團
     the process of assessing the detailed impact of HKFRS 18 on the                  正在評估香港財務報告準則第18號對本
     Group’s consolidated financial statements.                                       集團綜合財務報表的詳細影響。

            average method. Net realisable value represents the                           平均法計算。可變現淨值指存貨
            estimated selling price for inventories less all estimated costs              估計售價減估計完成所需之全部
            of completion and costs necessary to make the sale. Costs                     成本以及銷售所需之成本。促成
            necessary to make the sale include incremental costs directly                 銷售所需的成本包括銷售直接應
            attributable to the sale and non-incremental costs which the                  佔的增量成本和本集團為進行銷
            Group must incur to make the sale.                                            售而必須產生的非增量成本。

                    (ii) the borrower has a strong capacity to meet its                           險偏低，(ii)借方有強大

                    and (iii) adverse changes in economic and business                            現金流量責任，及(iii)較
                    conditions in the longer term may, but will not                               長期的經濟及業務狀況
                    necessarily, reduce the ability of the borrower to                            存在不利變動，未必削
                    fulfil its contractual cash flow obligations. The Group                       弱借方履行其合約現金
                    considers a debt instrument to have low credit                                流量義務的能力，則債
                    risk when it has an internal or external credit rating                        務工具的信貸風險會被
                    of “investment grade” as per globally understood                              釐定為偏低。當債務工
                    definitions.                                                                  具的內部或外部信貸評
                                                                                                  級為「投資級別」 （按照全

      JUDGEMENTS AND KEY SOURCES OF                                                    明朗因素之主要來源
      ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY
      In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are                 在應用本集團之會計政策（於附註3載
      described in Note 3, the Directors are required to make judgements,              述）過程中，董事須就未能即時自其他
      estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets                   來源取得資產及負債賬面值，作出判
      and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.                斷、估計及假設。該等估計及相關假設
      The estimates and underlying assumptions are based on historical                 按照過往經驗及其他被視為相關的其他
      experience and other factors that are considered to be relevant.                 因素釐定。實際結果可能有別於該等估
      Actual results may differ from these estimates.                                  計。

      The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-                  估計及相關假設會持續審閱。如會計估
      going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in the             計的修改僅影響該期間，則在修改期間
      period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only that        確認；如修改影響現時及未來期間，則
      period, or in the period of the revision and future periods if revision          在修改期間及未來期間均會確認。
      affects both current and future periods.

      of applying the Group’s accounting policies and that have the most               合財務報表內確認之款額造成最重大影
      significant effect on the amounts recognised in consolidated financial           響的重大判斷。
      statements.

      Makers (“CODM”), in order to allocate resources to segments and                     估其表現上所定期審閱的有關本集團不
      to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s reportable                   同部門之內部報告作為確定經營分部之
      segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into six main operating                        基準。具體而言，在香港財務報告準則
      divisions – (i) laminates, (ii) PCBs, (iii) chemicals, (iv) properties, (v)         第8號下，本集團之申報分部分為六個

      including service income, manufacture and sale of magnetic products                （主要包括按公平值計入其他全面收益
      and hotel business).                                                                的債務工具及按公平值計入損益的權益

9.    INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                               9.   所得稅開支（續）
      Under the Law of the PRC on EIT (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation                按照「中國企業所得稅法」
                                                                                                   （「企業所得稅
      Regulation of the EIT Law, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries is 25%            法」）及實施條例，兩個年度於中國附屬
      for both years.                                                                   公司之企業所得稅稅率為25%。

      Under the EIT Law of the PRC, withholding tax of 5% – 10% is                      根據中國企業所得稅法，自二零零八年
      imposed on dividends declared in respect of profits earned by                     一月一日起，就在中國國內附屬公司
      subsidiaries in Mainland China from 1 January 2008 onwards.                       所賺取的利潤所宣派的股息徵收5%至
                                                                                        10%的預提稅。

      Pursuant to the EIT Law, a High-New Technology Enterprise shall be                根據企業所得稅法，高新技術企業可自
      entitled to a preferential tax rate of 15% for three years since it was           其獲官方認可起享有為期三年的15%稅
      officially endorsed. Certain subsidiaries of the Company in the PRC               率優惠。本公司若干中國附屬公司獲官
      obtained official endorsement as a High-New Technology Enterprise                 方認定為高新技術企業（「稅務優惠」），
      (“Tax Concession”) and with the expiry dates on or before 2028 (2024:             到期日為二零二八年或之前（二零二四
      2026).                                                                            年：二零二六年）。

      The provision of LAT is estimated according to the requirements                   計提土地增值稅撥備是按有關中國稅法
      set forth in the relevant PRC tax laws and regulations. LAT has                   及規則要求估算。按土地增值金額（根
      been levied at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% on the                   據物業銷售收入扣去指定直接成本）以
      appreciation of land value, represented by the excess of sales                    累進稅率30%至60%基準繳交土地增值
      proceeds of properties over prescribed direct costs. Prescribed                   稅。指定直接成本界定為土地成本，發
      direct costs are defined to include costs of land, development and                展及建築成本，及其他關於房產發展的
      construction costs, as well as certain costs relating to the property             成本。按照國家稅務總局之官方公告，
      development. According to the State Administration of Taxation’s                  銷售物業時應暫繳土地增值稅，到房產
      official circulars, LAT shall be payable provisionally upon sales of              發展完成後才確認所得收益。
      the properties, followed by final ascertainment of the gain at the
      completion of the properties development.

      Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime of Hong Kong Profits                根據利得稅兩級制或香港利得稅制度，
      Tax, the first HK$2 million of profits of the qualifying group entity will        合資格集團實體的首二百萬港元利潤將
      be taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HK$2 million will be taxed at                按8.25%徵稅，而超過二百萬港元的利
      16.5%. The profits of group entities not qualifying for the two-tiered            潤則須按16.5%徵稅。不符合利得稅兩
      profits tax rates regime will continue to be taxed at a flat rate of              級制的集團實體的利潤將繼續按16.5%
      16.5%. Accordingly, the Hong Kong Profits Tax of the qualifying group             的固定稅率徵稅。因此，合資格集團
      entity is calculated at 8.25% on the first HK$2 million of the estimated          實體首二百萬港元估計應課稅溢利將
      assessable profits and at 16.5% on the estimated assessable profits               按 8.25% 的 稅 率 繳 納 香 港 利 得 稅 ， 而
      above HK$2 million.                                                               二百萬港元以上之估計應課稅溢利則按

14. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES (continued)                                              14. 投資物業（續）
      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the Directors        為釐定相關物業的公平值，董事已成立
      have set up a valuation committee, which is headed up by the                   估值委員會，由負責本集團財務事務的
      persons in charge of financial matters of the Group, to determine              人士帶領，以就公平值計量釐定適當的
      the appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value                 估值技術及輸入數據。
      measurements.

      The Group engages an independent qualified valuer to perform                   本集團委聘獨立合資格估值師估值。估
      its valuations. The valuation committee works closely with the                 值委員會與獨立合資格估值師緊密合
      independent qualified valuer to establish the appropriate valuation            作，共同制定模型之適當估值技術及輸
      techniques and inputs to the model. The persons in charge of financial         入數據。財務事宜負責人定期向董事匯
      matters report the valuation committee’s findings to the Directors             報估值委員會之發現，闡明資產公平值
      periodically to explain the cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the     波動之因由。
      assets.

      The Group’s investment properties were classified as Level 3 of the            本集團投資物業於二零二五年及二零
      fair value hierarchy as at 31 December 2025 and 2024.                          二四年十二月三十一日分類為第三級公
                                                                                     平值架構級別。

      The fair value of the Group’s investment properties at 31 December             本集團投資物業於二零二五年及二零
      2025 and 2024 had been arrived at on the basis of a valuation carried          二四年十二月三十一日之公平值乃按與
      out by Ravia Global Appraisal Advisory Limited, an independent                 本集團並無關連之獨立合資格估值師瑞
      qualified valuer not connected to the Group. Ravia Global Appraisal            豐環球評估諮詢有限公司進行估值之基
      Advisory Limited is a member of the Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors.          準達致。瑞豐環球評估諮詢有限公司為
      The valuation was determined based on direct comparison method                 香港測量師學會會員。估值乃按直接比
      by reference to the market transactions of similar properties taking           較法參考類似物業之市場交易並考慮參
      in consideration of timing of reference transactions, and property-            考交易的時間及物業特定的調整因素後
      specific adjustment factors. There has been no change from the                 釐定。於過往年度所用之估值技術並無
      valuation technique used in prior years.                                       變動。

      The following table gives information about how the fair values                 下表載列該等投資物業公平值的釐定方
      of these investment properties are determined (in particular, the               法（尤其是所使用估值技術及輸入數據）
      valuation technique and inputs used), as well as the fair value                 以及按公平值計量輸入數據的可觀察程
      hierarchy into which the fair value measurements are categorised                度將公平值計量分類之公平值架構級別
      (Level 1 to 3) based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair value       （第一至三級）。
      measurements is observable.

      The key assumptions are determined based on the unit’s past                        主要假設乃按該單位過往表現及管理層
      performance and the management’s expectations for the market                       對市場發展之期望及用作估算首五年

      not exceed the long-term average growth rates for the relevant                     長期增長率，而且增長率介乎0%至3%
      industries and growth rates ranging from 0% to 3% (2024: 0% to                     （二零二四年：0%至3%）。現金流量預
      3%). The cash flow projections, growth rates and pre-tax discount                  測、增長率和稅前貼現率已於二零二五

      into consideration higher degree of estimation uncertainties in the                估，計及各自年度因行業及經濟復甦程
      respective year due to how the recovery of industry and economy.                   度而導致的較高程度的估計不確定性。

      Since the recoverable amounts of each group of CGUs were above                       由於各組現金產生單位之可收回金額高

      its indebtedness, the Directors considered there was a significant             利息及本金，董事認為該發行人發行的
      increase in credit risk in the debt instruments at FVTOCI issued by            按公平值計入其他全面收益之債務工具
      this issuer. The Group engaged an independent qualified valuer to              的信貸風險大幅增加。本集團委聘獨立
      assist in the estimation of the ECL provision on the debt instruments          合資格估值師，透過釐定合適的違約概
      at FVTOCI issued by this issuer by determining an appropriate                  率、各債務工具的估計違約損失率及前
      probability of default rate, the estimated loss given default rate of each     瞻性調整，協助估計該發行人發行的按
      debt instrument and forward-looking adjustments.                               公平值計入其他全面收益的債務工具之
                                                                                     預期信貸虧損撥備。

      The investments in listed bonds are held by the Group within a                 對上市債券的投資均由本集團於目的為
      business model whose objective is both to collect their contractual            收取純粹為本金及尚未償還本金的利息
      cash flows which are solely payments of principal and interest on              付款之合約現金流量以及出售該等金融
      the principal amount outstanding and to sell these financial assets.           資產的業務模式內所持有。因此，對上
      Hence, the investments in listed bonds are classified as at FVTOCI.            市債券的投資分類為按公平值計入其他
                                                                                     全面收益。

      An impairment loss of HK$702,158,000 under expected credit                     截至二零二四年十二月三十一日止年
      loss model was recognised in profit or loss during year ended 31               度，預期信貸虧損模式項下減值虧損

34. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                     34. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will     資本風險管理本集團管理其資本以確保
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to       通過優化債務與股本結存為權益持有人
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.       爭取最高回報，使本集團旗下實體能夠
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.             持續經營。本集團之整體策略與過往年
                                                                                  度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings              誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity          之資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share              （扣除現金及現金等價物）及本公司持有
      capital, share premium, reserves and retained profits as disclosed in       人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢
      the consolidated statement of changes in equity.                            價、儲備及保留溢利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.          董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and      部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the      別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through     將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as           份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.           衡整體資本結構。

            The Group is exposed to equity and debt price risk through                  本集團因投資於按公平值計入損

            Stock Exchange, the management manages this exposure by                     易所及愛爾蘭證券交易所報價的
            maintaining a portfolio of investments with different risks. The            按公平值計入損益計量的權益工

            Credit risk and impairment assessment                                     信貸風險及減值評估
            Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties            信貸風險指本集團交易對手因違
            default on their contractual obligations resulting in financial           反其合約義務而導致本集團產生
            losses to the Group. The Group’s maximum exposure                         財務虧損的風險。倘交易方於報
            to credit risk in the event of the counterparties’ failure to             告期末未能履行彼等就各類已確
            perform their obligations at the end of the reporting period              認金融資產之承擔，則本集團須
            in relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the           承受之最高信貸風險為於綜合財
            carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated             務狀況表所載該等資產賬面值。
            statement of financial position.

            Trade receivables arising from contracts with customer                    由客戶合約產生的貿易應收賬款
            In order to minimise the credit risk, the Directors have                  為將信貸風險降至最低，董事已
            delegated the management to be responsible for                            委派管理層負責制訂信貸限額、
            determination of credit limits, credit approvals and other                信貸審批及其他監控程序，以確
            monitoring procedures to ensure that follow-up action is                  保採取跟進措施收回逾期未付之
            taken to recover overdue debts. Before accepting any new                  債項。在接納任何新客戶前，本
            customer, the Group uses an internal credit scoring system                集團運用內部信貸評分制度評估
            to assess the potential customer’s credit quality and defines             潛在客戶之信用質素，並按客戶
            credit limits by customer. The Group monitors any changes                 釐定信貸限額。自早前授出信貸
            in the credit quality of the trade receivables since the credit           起至報告期間結束為止，本集團
            was granted and up to the end of the reporting period. Limits             監察貿易應收賬款信用質素之任
            and scoring attributed to customers are reviewed twice a                  何變動。客戶之限額及評分每年
            year. Other monitoring procedures are in place to ensure that             審閱兩次。本集團亦設有其他監
            follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts. In this               控程序，以確保採取跟進措施收
            regard, the Directors consider that the Group’s credit risk is            回逾期未付之債項。就此而言，
            significantly reduced.                                                    董事認為，本集團之信貸風險已
                                                                                      大幅降低。

            The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk                 本集團並無高度集中之貿易應收
            on trade receivables with exposure spread over a number                   賬款信貸風險，風險由多個客戶
            of customers. However, the Group is exposed to the                        分攤。然而，本集團的風險集中
            concentration on geographic segment in the PRC. At 31                     於中國之地區分部。於二零二五
            December 2025, approximately 82% (2024: 95%) of the                       年十二月三十一日，本集團約
            Group’s trade receivables are arising from the PRC.                       82%（二零二四年：95%）之貿易
                                                                                      應收賬款源自中國。

            Except for credit-impaired trade receivables which are                    除個別進行減值評估的信貸減值
            assessed for impairment individually, the remaining trade                 貿易應收賬款外，其餘貿易應收
            receivables are grouped based on shared credit risk                       賬款參照本集團內部信貸評級，
            characteristics by reference to the Group’s internal credit               基於共同信貸風險特徵加以分

            the Directors considered there was a significant increase                  付利息及本金，董事認為該借款

            Group engaged an independent qualified valuer to assist in                 加。本集團委聘獨立合資格估值

37. FINANCIAL GUARANTEES                                                          37. 財務擔保合約
    CONTRACTS
      The Group provided guarantees amounting to HK$1,408,000 (2024:                於二零二五年十二月三十一日，本集團
      HK$10,147,000) as at 31 December 2025 to facilitate mortgage                  為本集團所發展物業之買家之按揭銀行
      bank loans applications of purchasers of the properties that were             貸款申請提供1,408,000港元（二零二四
      developed by the Group.                                                       年：10,147,000港元）擔保。


      The guarantees are given to banks with respect to loans procured by           本集團就本集團所發展物業之買家取得
      the purchasers of properties that were developed by the Group. Such           之貸款向銀行提供擔保。該等擔保將於
      guarantees will be released by banks upon delivery of the properties          物業交收予買家及相關按揭物業登記完
      to the purchasers and completion of registration of the relevant              成時由銀行解除。董事認為，本集團該
      mortgage properties. In the opinion of the Directors, the fair values         等財務擔保合約的公平值於初始確認時
      of these financial guarantee contracts of the Group are insignificant         並不重大，且董事認為參與各方違約的
      at initial recognition and the Directors consider that the possibility of     機會極微。因此，於二零二五年及二零
      default of the parties involved is remote. Accordingly, no value has          二四年十二月三十一日，於擔保合約開
      been recognised at the inception of the guarantee contracts and at            始及報告期間結束時並無確認任何價
      the end of the reporting period as at 31 December 2025 and 2024.              值。

38. CONTINGENT LIABILITIES                                                                       38. 或然負債
